    Ability: @legitch. Image: @le_nuberto

    Fabled

  98. Galaxy Sim.

    Before the game starts, up to 25% of the script can be changed by a consensus majority of players.

    SCP-1190 is a computer simulation of the universe that enthralls anyone who uses it.

    • Due to the nature of BOTC, any given script may contain problematic or disliked characters that a particular group is willing to avoid.
    • By the power of democracy, before the game begins, up to 25% of the script can be changed if a majority of players agree together.
    • It is not up to the Storyteller to direct conversations on what characters should change, it is the player's will that does.
    • A majority of players means more than 50%, not 50%, or less.
    • 25% of the script does included 1 more character rounded up.
    • Empty character slots beyond the minimum 13/4/4/1 of standard games may also be changed.

    How to Run

    Before the game starts, declare that the Galaxy Sim. is in play, and that the Storyteller is open to any changes that players unanimously want. Players may start discussions amongst themselves regarding what they'd like to see this game, and if a majority of players agree on a specific singular change, it is implemented. Otherwise, it can also be used to change empty character slots.

    Note that this Fabled cannot be a fix-all for bad scripts, and is limited to only 25% being susceptible to changes.

    JSON

    
  {
    "id": "galaxysim_scp_fragmented_veil",
    "image": "https://www.bloodstar.xyz/p/legitch/SCP_Fragmented_Veil/galaxysim_scp_fragmented_veil.png",
    "setup": true,
    "name": "Galaxy Sim. █",
    "team": "fabled",
    "ability": "Before the game starts, up to 25% of the script can be changed by a consensus majority of players.",
    "attribution": "Ability: @legitch. Image: @le_nuberto"
  }

    Attribution

    Ability: @legitch. Image: @le_nuberto

    Fabled

  99. Tyranny!

    Each day, if no-one is executed, all players publicly choose a coin side, then one is flipped! If the Demon is correct, evil wins.

    SCP-1678 is an authoritarian parallel London located underground that suggests certain doom to those above that don't join their salvation.

    • Tyranny! forces players to start picking up the pace and get executions going, preventing in-activity.
    • When night falls, if nobody was executed today, all players play Klutz-Fallacy using a coin-flip, where the good team will gain information on who the Demon is/isn't, or lose on the spot.
    • When the Tyranny! activates, all players must publicly choose a coin side - heads or tails. Players are not allowed to make their choice privately.
    • After all players have made their decision, the Storyteller flips a coin and publicly reveals the result once it lands.
    • If the Demon's choice is the side the Storyteller's coin landed on, the evil team wins the game immediately.

    How to Run

    Each dusk, if no player was executed today, the Tyranny! activates - declare that all players must publicly choose a coin side. Note the decision the Demon makes with either the HEADS or TAILS reminder token marked by their character token. After getting all players' choices, obtain a coin and flip it. If the Demon's coin side guess is correct, the game ends and the evil team wins. Otherwise, the game continues. As with all Fableds, remember that this bypasses all mis-registration effects. If there are multiple Demons but only 1 alive (or just active) one, count that Demon only. If there are multiple Demons that are alive/active, all Demons must have their coin side correct for the evil team to win.

    JSON

    
  {
    "id": "tyranny_scp_fragmented_veil",
    "image": "https://www.bloodstar.xyz/p/legitch/SCP_Fragmented_Veil/tyranny_scp_fragmented_veil.png",
    "reminders": [
      "Heads",
      "Tails"
    ],
    "name": "Tyranny! █",
    "team": "fabled",
    "ability": "Each day, if no-one is executed, all players publicly choose a coin side, then one is flipped! If the Demon is correct, evil wins.",
    "attribution": "Ability: @legitch. Image: @le_nuberto"
  }

    Feel free to purposely exclude players from needing to make a decision depending on the on-script characters, such as excluding dead players if there is no possible way an active Demon can be dead and the game still continues (Ex. Zombuul).

    When playing online, where the visibility of the coin flip's outcome is likely invisible to other players, play honestly and genuinely flip a coin or use a coin-flipping website to determine the outcome, otherwise you impose no actual teeth or threat using this fabled to encourage executions.

    Optional rule: You may also choose to adjust the rules of this Fabled if good's goal to execute the Demon has been altered, such as the Lleech winning if their host's coin flip is correct as opposed to them.

    Optional rule: you may allow for players not to make a decision at all with what coin side they choose, which only affects the Demon and if they'll win or not. However, this goes against the original intent of the Tyranny!, and is not recommended unless you know what you are doing.

    Attribution

    Ability: @legitch. Image: @le_nuberto

    Fabled

  100. The Gift

    Players experiencing any hardships (in-game or in real life) might have something good happen to them or their ability.

    SCP-2536 is the gift that keeps on giving - pity (LOSER).

    • At any time, the Storyteller may do anything good to a player as a catch-up mechanic, or to help improve the general mood of the game.
    • The Gift's effect can do anything, which includes being silent about it's effects, telling just the effected player, or even telling every player publicly.
    • The Storyteller may also consider the player's emotions or state of affairs in regards to whether they should trigger this for them.

    How to Run

    At any time, if you notice that a player is not performing to well within the game, you may choose to do something good for them or their ability. You may need to mark their character token with a SOMETHING X reminder, to remind that they are now sober & healthy, or they may use their ability twice, or they have double the votes and potential to nominate, or simply as a reminder to decide on what to do later.

    JSON

  101. Dr. Maynard

    Before setup, the player designated to be Demon gets the choice of Demons they get to be.

    Dr. Maynard is a traitor to the foundation that enables all hell to be broken loose.

    • At the start of the game, a player is chosen to be the Demon, and gets a list of Demons they can choose on being.
    • This list may be between at-least 2 Demons to all Demons present on the script.
    • Any setup effects imposed by the Demon chosen will also be applied as normal.

    How to Run

    At the start of the game, declare that Dr. Maynard is in play. Add the Dr. Maynard token to the Grimoire.

    Before setting up the game, choose the player that will be the Demon, mark them with the DEMON reminder, and wake them. Show them the Demon character tokens they can choose. The Demon player points to a Demon character icon on their character sheet. That player is the Demon they chose - set them up with the new Demon character token on Grimoire and put a Townsfolk in the character bag instead of the Demon token. Adjust for any setup effects the Demon has. Put the Demon player to sleep.

    When sending out character tokens, when you reach the Demon player, take out a Townsfolk token instead - they already have their character token.

    JSON

    
  {
    "id": "drmaynard_scp_fragmented_veil",
    "image": "https://www.bloodstar.xyz/p/legitch/SCP_Fragmented_Veil/drmaynard_scp_fragmented_veil.png",
    "setup": true,
    "name": "Dr. Maynard █",

    "ability": "Before setup, the player designated to be Demon gets the choice of Demons they get to be.",

  }

    If the method of communication to the Demon is not reliable in doing silently and could compromise their identity, feel free to choose another method to achieve the same effect, such as silently texting the player or talking to them before the game even begins.

    Fabled

  102. W's Library

    Successful kills after the 1st each night instead poison the victim from now on. Day kills are delayed until night.

    The Wanderer's Library is a safehaven of books that prevents there being more than 1 recognizable kill.

    • Each night, only the 1st kill on a player will cause them to die - further kills made that night instead indefinitely poison them from now on.
    • Kills prevented by any protection effect don't register for this Fabled, neither as the 1st kill, or any subsequent ones that would poison instead.
    • Players poisoned by this Fabled will continue to be poisoned, even if the killer is dead or they gain a healthiness effect.
    • However, players affected whilst currently healthy will remain unpoisoned until they are vulnerable for any amount of time afterwards.
    • If a player would die during the day due to another player's ability, that death is delayed until after any possible Demon or Minion kills.

    How to Run

    If any player would die tonight but there is a DEAD reminder present on another player, that player is instead poisoned - mark them with the POISONED reminder, unless that player's ability makes them permanently healthy without any conditions (if a player is made temporarily healthy by an ability, still mark them with the reminder, but don't treat them as permanently-poisoned until they lose that healthiness).

    If any player would die to any player's ability during the day, they remain alive. Mark their character token with the DEAD reminder token. Tonight, the player marked with this DEAD reminder dies (or is poisoned depending on circumstances).

    "id": "wslibrary_scp_fragmented_veil",
    "image": "https://www.bloodstar.xyz/p/legitch/SCP_Fragmented_Veil/wslibrary_scp_fragmented_veil.png",
    "otherNightReminder": "If a day kill was delayed: those marked with 'Dead' die or are poisoned if another player is dieing tonight.",
